Implement the following plan:

# Fix Gemini Structured Output Repetition Loop

## Context

Production is producing repeating digit artifacts like `"2202202020202020..."` in field mapping values. Root cause investigation confirmed:

- **Source**: 100% from `gemini_field_mapping` spans — `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI.with_structured_output(FieldMappingResponse)`
- **NOT** MedGemma, structure_builder, or ordinal_resolver
- **Frequency**: 10 out of 547 field_mapping spans (1.8%), concentrated on compound criteria
- **Root cause**: Optional Pydantic fields with `default=None` produce JSON schemas without `required`, which triggers the known Gemini repetition loop bug ([cookbook#449](https://github.com/google-gemini/cookbook/issues/449))
- **DRY problem**: 5 identical `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(...)` instantiations across 3 files — only `create_structured_llm()` would get the fix, leaving 4 inline usages unprotected

## Fix: 3 Layers

### Layer 1: Consolidate All Gemini Structured Output Through `create_structured_llm()`

Currently 5 places create `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` with identical params:

| File | Function | Schema | Currently uses `create_structured_llm()`? |
|------|----------|--------|------------------------------------------|
| `gemini_utils.py:138` | `create_structured_llm()` | any | YES (is the utility) |
| `medgemma_decider.py:145` | `_structure_decision_with_gemini()` | `GroundingDecision` | NO — inline |
| `medgemma_decider.py:311` | `_structure_reasoning_with_gemini()` | `AgenticReasoningResult` | NO — inline |
| `entity_decomposer.py:119` | `decompose_entities()` | `DecomposedEntityList` | NO — inline |
| `entity_decomposer.py:224` | `medgemma_decompose_entities()` | `DecomposedEntityList` | NO — inline |

**Change**: Replace all 4 inline `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(...)` + `.with_structured_output()` calls with `create_structured_llm(Schema)`. This ensures all Gemini structured output calls get the same parameters, Ollama fallback, and future fixes.

Each callsite handles `None` return (no LLM backend) by raising or returning an empty result, which matches existing error handling patterns.

### Layer 2: Fix Generation Parameters in `create_structured_llm()`

Add temperature and repetition penalties — applies to ALL 5 callsites via the consolidation:

```python
gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
    model=gemini_model_name,
    google_api_key=google_api_key,
    max_output_tokens=2048,
    temperature=0.1,                          # was: unset (default ~1.0)
    model_kwargs={
        "frequency_penalty": 0.8,             # penalize token repetition
        "presence_penalty": 0.5,              # encourage topic diversity
    },
)
```

### Layer 3: Make All Pydantic Schema Fields Required

Change `default=None` / `default_factory=list` to `Field(...)` so ALL fields appear in JSON schema `required` arrays:

**`FieldMappingValue`** — `required: ["type"]` -> `required: ["type", "value", "unit", "min", "max", "duration"]`
**`FieldMappingItem`** — add `unit`, `value_concept_id`, `value_concept_system` to required
**`FieldMappingResponse`** — `required: []` -> `required: ["mappings"]`

```python
# BEFORE:
value: str | None = Field(default=None, description="...")
# AFTER:
value: str | None = Field(..., description="...")
```

### Layer 4: Safety Net — Detection + Retry (already implemented)

Keep the existing defenses in `gemini_utils.py` and `field_mapper.py`:
- `is_repetition_loop()` detection
- `check_model_for_repetition()` recursive inspector
- `sanitize_repetition_artifacts` Pydantic validator
- 3-attempt retry loop in `generate_field_mappings()`

## Files to Modify

| File | Changes |
|------|---------|
| `services/.../tools/gemini_utils.py` | Add `temperature=0.1`, `frequency_penalty`, `presence_penalty` to `create_structured_llm()`. Keep detection/retry code. |
| `services/.../tools/field_mapper.py` | Make all Pydantic fields required (`Field(...)` instead of `Field(default=None)`) |
| `services/.../tools/medgemma_decider.py` | Replace 2 inline `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` blocks with `create_structured_llm()`. Remove `from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI`. |
| `services/.../tools/entity_decomposer.py` | Replace 2 inline `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` blocks with `create_structured_llm()`. Remove inline import. |

## Verification

1. Print new JSON schemas — confirm ALL fields in `required` arrays
2. `uv run python tests/e2e/test_repetition_detection.py` — unit tests pass
3. Run compound criterion reproduction test 5x — confirm no repeating digits
4. `uv run ruff check` on all 4 modified files
5. Commit production changes

     1→"""Shared Gemini LLM utilities for protocol processor tools.
     2→
     3→Centralizes the repeated pattern of creating a ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
     4→client with structured output and parsing its response. Used by
     5→structure_builder, ordinal_resolver, and field_mapper.
     6→
     7→Falls back to Ollama when GOOGLE_API_KEY is not set but OLLAMA_BASE_URL
     8→is configured.
     9→
    10→Includes detection for a known Gemini structured-output bug where the
    11→model enters a token repetition loop, producing repeating digits or
    12→regurgitated schema descriptions instead of actual values.  See:
    13→  - https://github.com/google-gemini/cookbook/issues/449
    14→  - https://github.com/googleapis/python-genai/issues/1039
    15→"""
    16→
    17→from __future__ import annotations
    18→
    19→import logging
    20→import os
    21→import re
    22→from typing import Any, TypeVar
    23→
    24→from pydantic import BaseModel
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→# Pattern that detects schema description regurgitation — the model
    29→# outputs Pydantic field descriptions or chain-of-thought reasoning
    30→# instead of actual values.
    31→_SCHEMA_LEAK_RE = re.compile(
    32→    r"(?:"
    33→    r"Unit of measurement"
    34→    r"|Value for standard type"
    35→    r"|Duration value for"
    36→    r"|measurement unit"
    37→    r"|provided schema"
    38→    r"|In the context of"
    39→    r"|populate the .+ field"
    40→    r"|will populate"
    41→    r")",
    42→    re.IGNORECASE,
    43→)
    44→
    45→
    46→def is_repetition_loop(text: str | None) -> bool:
    47→    """Detect if text contains a Gemini repetition loop.
    48→
    49→    Known Gemini bug: the model enters a token repetition loop during
    50→    structured output generation, producing sequences like
    51→    ``"220220220220220220"`` or ``"8598739459392231e-315"`` repeated
    52→    until ``max_output_tokens`` is exhausted.
    53→
    54→    Returns True if the text is likely a repetition artifact.
    55→    """
    56→    if not text or len(text) < 20:
    57→        return False
    58→
    59→    # Check 1: very low character diversity for the string length
    60→    unique_ratio = len(set(text)) / len(text)
    61→    if unique_ratio < 0.08 and len(text) > 30:
    62→        return True
    63→
    64→    # Check 2: repeating substring (3-30 chars repeated 3+ times)
    65→    # Tries from position 0 first, then from later offsets to catch
    66→    # repetition that starts after a short prefix.
    67→    max_plen = min(31, len(text) // 3 + 1)
    68→    for start in range(0, min(20, len(text) // 2)):
    69→        remaining = text[start:]
    70→        if len(remaining) < 9:
    71→            break
    72→        for plen in range(3, min(max_plen, len(remaining) // 3 + 1)):
    73→            pattern = remaining[:plen]
    74→            count = 0
    75→            for i in range(0, len(remaining) - plen + 1, plen):
    76→                if remaining[i : i + plen] == pattern:
    77→                    count += 1
    78→                else:
    79→                    break
    80→            if count >= 3 and count * plen >= len(remaining) * 0.7:
    81→                return True
    82→
    83→    # Check 3: schema description regurgitation
    84→    if _SCHEMA_LEAK_RE.search(text):
    85→        return True
    86→
    87→    return False
    88→
    89→class RepetitionLoopError(RuntimeError):
    90→    """Raised when a Gemini structured-output response contains repetition artifacts."""
    91→
    92→
    93→def check_model_for_repetition(model: BaseModel) -> list[str]:
    94→    """Recursively inspect all string fields of a Pydantic model for repetition.
    95→
    96→    Returns a list of field paths that contain repetition artifacts,
    97→    e.g. ["mappings[0].value.unit", "mappings[1].value.value"].
    98→    """
    99→    bad_fields: list[str] = []
   100→
   101→    def _check(obj: Any, prefix: str) -> None:
   102→        if isinstance(obj, BaseModel):
   103→            for field_name in obj.model_fields:
   104→                _check(getattr(obj, field_name, None), f"{prefix}.{field_name}")
   105→        elif isinstance(obj, list):
   106→            for i, item in enumerate(obj):
   107→                _check(item, f"{prefix}[{i}]")
   108→        elif isinstance(obj, str) and is_repetition_loop(obj):
   109→            bad_fields.append(prefix)
   110→
   111→    _check(model, "root")
   112→    return bad_fields
   113→
   114→
   115→T = TypeVar("T", bound=BaseModel)
   116→
   117→
   118→def create_structured_llm(
   119→    output_schema: type[T],
   120→) -> Any | None:
   121→    """Create an LLM client with structured output.
   122→
   123→    Tries Gemini first (GOOGLE_API_KEY), falls back to Ollama
   124→    (OLLAMA_BASE_URL) if Gemini is not configured.
   125→
   126→    Args:
   127→        output_schema: Pydantic model class for structured output.
   128→
   129→    Returns:
   130→        A structured LLM instance, or None if no backend is available.
   131→    """
   132→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   133→    if google_api_key:
   134→        try:
   135→            from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
   136→
   137→            gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   138→            gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   139→                model=gemini_model_name,
   140→                google_api_key=google_api_key,
   141→                max_output_tokens=2048,
   142→            )
   143→            return gemini.with_structured_output(output_schema)
   144→        except Exception as e:
   145→            logger.warning("Failed to create Gemini client: %s", e)
   146→
   147→    # Fallback to Ollama
   148→    ollama_base_url = os.getenv("OLLAMA_BASE_URL")
   149→    if ollama_base_url:
   150→        try:
   151→            from langchain_community.chat_models import ChatOllama
   152→
   153→            ollama_model = os.getenv("OLLAMA_MODEL", "gemma2:9b")
   154→            logger.info(
   155→                "Using Ollama for structured output: %s/%s",
   156→                ollama_base_url,
   157→                ollama_model,
   158→            )
   159→            ollama = ChatOllama(
   160→                base_url=ollama_base_url,
   161→                model=ollama_model,
   162→                format="json",
   163→                temperature=0.1,
   164→            )
   165→            return ollama.with_structured_output(output_schema)
   166→        except Exception as e:
   167→            logger.warning("Failed to create Ollama client: %s", e)
   168→
   169→    logger.warning("No LLM backend available — skipping structured output call")
   170→    return None
   171→
   172→
   173→def parse_structured_output(result: Any, model: type[T]) -> T:
   174→    """Parse a structured LLM output into a Pydantic model.
   175→
   176→    Handles both dict and already-parsed model instances returned
   177→    by LangChain's with_structured_output().
   178→
   179→    Args:
   180→        result: Raw LLM output (dict or model instance).
   181→        model: Pydantic model class to validate against.
   182→
   183→    Returns:
   184→        Validated model instance.
   185→    """
   186→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   187→        return model.model_validate(result)
   188→    return result  # type: ignore[no-any-return]
   189→
```

     1→"""Field mapping generation tool for grounded entities.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "Generate suggested field_mappings during grounding (ground node)"
     4→Per CONTEXT.md: "Criteria should be decomposed per AutoCriteria pattern: separate
     5→Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time"

     7→medical expert"
     8→
     9→Uses Gemini to generate suggested field mappings for the grounded entity +
    10→criterion text. These are best-effort suggestions — reviewer can edit in UI.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import logging
    16→from typing import Any, Literal
    17→
    18→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, field_validator, model_validator
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.prompts import render_template
    21→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    22→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
    23→    check_model_for_repetition,
    24→    create_structured_llm,
    25→    is_repetition_loop,
    26→    parse_structured_output,
    27→)
    28→
    29→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    30→
    31→# Mapping from legacy/LLM relation operators to the frontend's RelationOperator set
    32→_RELATION_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    33→    "has": "contains",
    34→    "is": "=",
    35→    "not": "not_contains",
    36→    "==": "=",
    37→    "range": "within",
    38→}
    39→
    40→
    41→def _normalize_relation(rel: str) -> str:
    42→    """Normalize a relation operator to the frontend's accepted set.
    43→
    44→    Maps legacy operators (has, is, not, ==, range) to the standard set:
    45→    =, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within, not_in_last, contains, not_contains.
    46→    """
    47→    return _RELATION_MAP.get(rel, rel)
    48→
    49→
    50→class FieldMappingValue(BaseModel):
    51→    """Typed value object for field mappings with type discriminator.
    52→
    53→    Supports three value shapes:
    54→    - standard: single value + unit (e.g. HbA1c < 7%)
    55→    - range: min/max + unit (e.g. Age 18-65 years)
    56→    - temporal: duration + unit (e.g. within 6 months)
    57→    """
    58→
    59→    type: Literal["standard", "range", "temporal"] = Field(
    60→        description="Value type discriminator"
    61→    )
    62→    value: str | None = Field(
    63→        default=None, description="Value for standard type (e.g. '7')"
    64→    )
    65→    unit: str | None = Field(
    66→        default=None, description="Unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'months')"
    67→    )
    68→    min: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Minimum value for range type")
    69→    max: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Maximum value for range type")
    70→    duration: str | None = Field(
    71→        default=None, description="Duration value for temporal type"
    72→    )
    73→
    74→    @model_validator(mode="after")
    75→    def sanitize_repetition_artifacts(self) -> "FieldMappingValue":
    76→        """Guard against Gemini repetition loops in values."""
    77→        _max = 200
    78→        for attr in ("value", "unit", "min", "max", "duration"):
    79→            val = getattr(self, attr)
    80→            if not isinstance(val, str):
    81→                continue
    82→            # Null out values that are repetition artifacts
    83→            if is_repetition_loop(val):
    84→                setattr(self, attr, None)
    85→            elif len(val) > _max:
    86→                setattr(self, attr, val[:_max])
    87→        return self
    88→
    89→
    90→# The set of valid relation operators accepted by the frontend
    91→RelationOperator = Literal[
    92→    "=", "!=", ">", ">=", "<", "<=", "within", "not_in_last", "contains", "not_contains"
    93→]
    94→
    95→
    96→class FieldMappingItem(BaseModel):
    97→    """A single AutoCriteria field mapping decomposition.
    98→
    99→    Per the AutoCriteria pattern, each criterion is decomposed into
   100→    separate Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, and Time components.
   101→    """
   102→
   103→    entity: str = Field(description="The medical entity name (e.g. 'HbA1c')")
   104→    relation: RelationOperator = Field(
   105→        description="The logical operator/relation (e.g. '<', '>', '=', 'contains')"
   106→    )
   107→    value: FieldMappingValue = Field(
   108→        description="Typed value object with type discriminator"
   109→    )
   110→    unit: str | None = Field(
   111→        default=None,
   112→        description="Optional unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')",
   113→    )
   114→    value_concept_id: str | None = Field(
   115→        default=None,
   116→        description="OMOP concept ID for categorical values",
   117→    )
   118→    value_concept_system: str | None = Field(
   119→        default=None,
   120→        description="Terminology system for value_concept_id (e.g. 'SNOMED', 'OMOP')",
   121→    )
   122→
   123→    @field_validator("relation", mode="before")
   124→    @classmethod
   125→    def normalize_relation(cls, v: str) -> str:
   126→        """Normalize LLM-generated relation operators before Literal validation."""
   127→        if isinstance(v, str):
   128→            return _normalize_relation(v)
   129→        return v
   130→
   131→
   132→class FieldMappingResponse(BaseModel):
   133→    """Gemini structured output for field mappings."""
   134→
   135→    mappings: list[FieldMappingItem] = Field(
   136→        default_factory=list,
   137→        description="List of AutoCriteria field mapping decompositions",
   138→    )
   139→
   140→
   141→async def generate_field_mappings(
   142→    entity: EntityGroundingResult,
   143→    criterion_text: str,
   144→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   145→    """Generate suggested field mappings for a grounded entity.
   146→
   147→    Uses Gemini to decompose the criterion text into AutoCriteria field mappings:
   148→    Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time components for each discrete condition.
   149→
   150→    This is a best-effort suggestion — reviewer can edit in the UI. Errors
   151→    are logged and an empty list returned (not propagated as failures).
   152→
   153→    Args:
   154→        entity: Grounded EntityGroundingResult with code and preferred term.
   155→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   156→
   157→    Returns:
   158→        List of field mapping dicts with keys: entity, relation, value,
   159→        entity_code, entity_system, omop_concept_id, entity_type.
   160→        Empty list if generation fails.
   161→    """
   162→    if not criterion_text:
   163→        return []
   164→
   165→    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(FieldMappingResponse)
   166→    if structured_llm is None:
   167→        return []
   168→
   169→    # Build a context-rich prompt for field mapping generation
   170→    grounded_term = entity.preferred_term or entity.entity_text
   171→    code_context = ""
   172→    if entity.selected_code and entity.selected_system:
   173→        system = entity.selected_system.upper()
   174→        code_context = f"(grounded to {system} code: {entity.selected_code})"
   175→
   176→    prompt = render_template(
   177→        "field_mapping.jinja2",
   178→        grounded_term=grounded_term,
   179→        code_context=code_context,
   180→        criterion_text=criterion_text,
   181→        entity_type=entity.entity_type or "Condition",
   182→    )
   183→
   184→    max_attempts = 3
   185→    for attempt in range(1, max_attempts + 1):
   186→        try:
   187→            from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   188→
   189→            with llm_span("gemini_field_mapping") as llm:
   190→                llm.set_request(prompt)
   191→                result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   192→                llm.set_response(str(result))
   193→
   194→            response = parse_structured_output(result, FieldMappingResponse)
   195→
   196→            # Check for Gemini repetition loop artifacts in the response
   197→            bad_fields = check_model_for_repetition(response)
   198→            if bad_fields:
   199→                if attempt < max_attempts:
   200→                    logger.warning(
   201→                        "Repetition loop detected in field mapping for '%s' "
   202→                        "(attempt %d/%d, fields: %s) — retrying",
   203→                        entity.entity_text[:50],
   204→                        attempt,
   205→                        max_attempts,
   206→                        bad_fields,
   207→                    )
   208→                    continue
   209→                logger.warning(
   210→                    "Repetition loop persisted after %d attempts for '%s' "
   211→                    "(fields: %s) — returning sanitized result",
   212→                    max_attempts,
   213→                    entity.entity_text[:50],
   214→                    bad_fields,
   215→                )
   216→
   217→            mappings = [
   218→                {
   219→                    "entity": m.entity,
   220→                    "relation": m.relation,
   221→                    "value": m.value.model_dump(exclude_none=True),
   222→                    "entity_code": entity.selected_code,
   223→                    "entity_system": entity.selected_system,
   224→                    "omop_concept_id": entity.omop_concept_id,
   225→                    "entity_type": entity.entity_type,
   226→                    "value_concept_id": m.value_concept_id,
   227→                    "value_concept_system": m.value_concept_system,
   228→                }
   229→                for m in response.mappings
   230→            ]
   231→
   232→            logger.info(
   233→                "Generated %d field mapping(s) for entity '%s'",
   234→                len(mappings),
   235→                entity.entity_text[:50],
   236→            )
   237→            return mappings
   238→
   239→        except Exception as e:
   240→            logger.warning(
   241→                "Field mapping generation failed for entity '%s' "
   242→                "(attempt %d/%d): %s",
   243→                entity.entity_text[:50],
   244→                attempt,
   245→                max_attempts,
   246→                e,
   247→                exc_info=True,
   248→            )
   249→            if attempt == max_attempts:
   250→                break
   251→
   252→    # Best-effort: return empty list on failure
   253→    return []
   254→

     1→"""MedGemma decision tool for best-match selection from TerminologyRouter candidates.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "MedGemma acts as decision-maker, minimum token usage."
     4→MedGemma evaluates candidates returned by TerminologyRouter and selects the best
     5→match for each entity. Uses the two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical
     6→reasoning, Gemini for structured output parsing.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import logging
    12→import os
    13→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
    14→
    15→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    16→    from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import TerminologyRouter
    17→
    18→from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, SystemMessage
    19→from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    20→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    21→
    22→from protocol_processor.prompts import render_template
    23→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import (
    24→    EntityGroundingResult,
    25→    GroundingCandidate,
    26→)
    27→
    28→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    29→
    30→_model_loader = None
    31→
    32→
    33→class AgenticReasoningResult(BaseModel):
    34→    """MedGemma's 3-question reasoning output for failed grounding retry.
    35→
    36→    Used by agentic_reasoning_loop to determine whether to skip an entity,
    37→    apply derived entity mapping, or rephrase the query for a better search.
    38→
    39→    Attributes:
    40→        should_skip: True if entity is not a valid medical criterion.
    41→        is_derived: True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept.
    42→        derived_term: Standard concept term if is_derived is True.
    43→        rephrased_query: Rephrased medical terminology query if applicable.
    44→        gemini_suggestion: Optional additional reformulation from Gemini.
    45→        reasoning: Brief explanation of the reasoning decisions.
    46→    """
    47→
    48→    should_skip: bool = Field(
    49→        default=False,
    50→        description=(
    51→            "True if entity is not a valid medical criterion "
    52→            "(e.g., consent, participation, willingness)"
    53→        ),
    54→    )
    55→    is_derived: bool = Field(
    56→        default=False,
    57→        description="True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept",
    58→    )
    59→    derived_term: str | None = Field(
    60→        default=None,
    61→        description=(
    62→            "Standard concept term if is_derived is True "
    63→            "(e.g., 'age' for 'age >= 18 years')"
    64→        ),
    65→    )
    66→    rephrased_query: str | None = Field(
    67→        default=None,
    68→        description=(
    69→            "Rephrased medical terminology query for better search "
    70→            "(e.g., 'hypertension' for 'high blood pressure')"
    71→        ),
    72→    )
    73→    gemini_suggestion: str | None = Field(
    74→        default=None,
    75→        description=("Optional reformulation suggestion from Gemini structuring step"),
    76→    )
    77→    reasoning: str = Field(
    78→        default="",
    79→        description="Brief explanation of the three-question reasoning",
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→class GroundingDecision(BaseModel):
    84→    """MedGemma's decision for the best terminology match."""
    85→
    86→    selected_code: str | None = Field(
    87→        default=None,
    88→        description=(
    89→            "Selected terminology code (CUI for UMLS, SNOMED code, etc)."
    90→            " Null if no good match."
    91→        ),
    92→    )
    93→    selected_system: str | None = Field(
    94→        default=None,
    95→        description=(
    96→            "The API/system that produced the selected code (e.g. 'umls', 'snomed')."
    97→        ),
    98→    )
    99→    preferred_term: str | None = Field(
   100→        default=None,
   101→        description="Canonical preferred term for the selected code.",
   102→    )
   103→    confidence: float = Field(
   104→        ge=0.0,
   105→        le=1.0,
   106→        description=(
   107→            "Confidence score 0.0-1.0."
   108→            " 0.9-1.0=exact, 0.7-0.8=synonym, 0.5-0.6=partial, 0.0=no match."
   109→        ),
   110→    )
   111→    reasoning: str = Field(
   112→        default="",
   113→        description="Brief explanation for selection.",
   114→    )
   115→
   116→
   117→def _get_medgemma_model() -> Any:
   118→    """Get or create MedGemma model instance."""
   119→    global _model_loader  # noqa: PLW0603
   120→    if _model_loader is None:
   121→        from inference.config import AgentConfig
   122→        from inference.model_garden import create_model_loader
   123→
   124→        _model_loader = create_model_loader(AgentConfig.from_env())
   125→    return _model_loader()
   126→
   127→
   128→async def _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> GroundingDecision:
   129→    """Structure raw MedGemma output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   130→
   131→    Args:
   132→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma output (free-form medical reasoning).
   133→
   134→    Returns:
   135→        GroundingDecision with selected code and confidence.
   136→    """
   137→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   138→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   139→
   140→    if not google_api_key:
   141→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   142→
   143→    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   144→
   145→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   146→        model=gemini_model_name,
   147→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   148→        max_output_tokens=2048,
   149→    )
   150→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(GroundingDecision)
   151→
   152→    prompt = render_template("structure_decision.jinja2", raw_text=raw_text)
   153→
   154→    with llm_span("gemini_structure_decision", gemini_model_name) as llm:
   155→        llm.set_request(prompt)
   156→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   157→        resp_text = str(result) if not isinstance(result, dict) else str(result)
   158→        llm.set_response(resp_text)
   159→
   160→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   161→        return GroundingDecision.model_validate(result)
   162→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   163→
   164→
   165→async def medgemma_decide(
   166→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   167→    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate],
   168→    criterion_context: str,
   169→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
   170→    """Use MedGemma to select the best terminology match for an entity.
   171→
   172→    MedGemma evaluates the candidates returned by TerminologyRouter and
   173→    selects the most appropriate code for the entity. Returns a result with
   174→    confidence=0.0 and no code if no candidates are available or none are
   175→    appropriate.
   176→
   177→    Args:
   178→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   179→        candidates: List of GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
   180→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   181→
   182→    Returns:
   183→        EntityGroundingResult with the selected code, confidence, and reasoning.
   184→    """
   185→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   186→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   187→    criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   188→
   189→    # If no candidates, return result with confidence=0.0
   190→    if not candidates:
   191→        logger.info(
   192→            "No candidates for entity '%s' (type=%s, criterion=%s)"
   193→            " — returning empty grounding",
   194→            entity_text[:50],
   195→            entity_type,
   196→            criterion_id[:12],
   197→        )
   198→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   199→            entity_text=entity_text,
   200→            entity_type=entity_type,
   201→            selected_code=None,
   202→            selected_system=None,
   203→            preferred_term=None,
   204→            confidence=0.0,
   205→            candidates=[],
   206→            reasoning="No terminology candidates available from any API",
   207→        )
   208→
   209→    try:
   210→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   211→
   212→        system_prompt = render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   213→        evaluate_prompt = render_template(
   214→            "grounding_evaluate.jinja2",
   215→            entity_text=entity_text,
   216→            entity_type=entity_type,
   217→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   218→            candidates=candidates,
   219→        )
   220→
   221→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   222→
   223→        model_name = getattr(model, "model_name", "") or getattr(model, "model", "")
   224→        messages = [
   225→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   226→            HumanMessage(content=evaluate_prompt),
   227→        ]
   228→
   229→        with llm_span("medgemma_evaluate", str(model_name)) as llm:
   230→            llm.set_request(f"[system] {system_prompt}\n\n[user] {evaluate_prompt}")
   231→            raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   232→            raw_text = raw_response.content
   233→            usage: dict[str, int] = {}
   234→            um = getattr(raw_response, "usage_metadata", None)
   235→            if isinstance(um, dict):
   236→                if um.get("input_tokens"):
   237→                    usage["input_tokens"] = um["input_tokens"]
   238→                if um.get("output_tokens"):
   239→                    usage["output_tokens"] = um["output_tokens"]
   240→                if um.get("total_tokens"):
   241→                    usage["total_tokens"] = um["total_tokens"]
   242→            llm.set_response(str(raw_text), usage or None)
   243→
   244→        logger.debug(
   245→            "MedGemma evaluate response for '%s' (first 200 chars): %s",
   246→            entity_text[:30],
   247→            raw_text[:200],
   248→        )
   249→
   250→        decision = await _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text)
   251→
   252→        logger.info(
   253→            "Grounding decision for '%s': code=%s, system=%s, conf=%.2f",
   254→            entity_text[:50],
   255→            decision.selected_code,
   256→            decision.selected_system,
   257→            decision.confidence,
   258→        )
   259→
   260→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   261→            entity_text=entity_text,
   262→            entity_type=entity_type,
   263→            selected_code=decision.selected_code,
   264→            selected_system=decision.selected_system,
   265→            preferred_term=decision.preferred_term,
   266→            confidence=decision.confidence,
   267→            candidates=candidates,
   268→            reasoning=decision.reasoning,
   269→        )
   270→
   271→    except Exception as e:
   272→        logger.error(
   273→            "MedGemma decision failed for entity '%s': %s",
   274→            entity_text[:50],
   275→            e,
   276→            exc_info=True,
   277→        )
   278→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   279→            entity_text=entity_text,
   280→            entity_type=entity_type,
   281→            selected_code=None,
   282→            selected_system=None,
   283→            preferred_term=None,
   284→            confidence=0.0,
   285→            candidates=candidates,
   286→            reasoning=f"MedGemma decision failed: {e}",
   287→        )
   288→
   289→
   290→async def _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   291→    """Structure raw MedGemma reasoning output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   292→
   293→    Gemini also acts as a collaborating agent here — it can add its own
   294→    reformulation suggestions via the gemini_suggestion field when structuring
   295→    the output.
   296→
   297→    Args:
   298→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma reasoning output (free-form text).
   299→
   300→    Returns:
   301→        AgenticReasoningResult with structured 3-question answers.
   302→    """
   303→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   304→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   305→
   306→    if not google_api_key:
   307→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   308→
   309→    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   310→
   311→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   312→        model=gemini_model_name,
   313→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   314→        max_output_tokens=2048,
   315→    )
   316→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(AgenticReasoningResult)
   317→
   318→    prompt = render_template("structure_reasoning.jinja2", raw_text=raw_text)
   319→
   320→    with llm_span("gemini_structure_reasoning", gemini_model_name) as llm:
   321→        llm.set_request(prompt)
   322→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   323→        llm.set_response(str(result))
   324→
   325→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   326→        return AgenticReasoningResult.model_validate(result)
   327→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   328→
   329→
   330→async def agentic_reasoning_loop(
   331→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   332→    criterion_context: str,
   333→    router: "TerminologyRouter",
   334→    attempt: int = 1,
   335→) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   336→    """Ask MedGemma 3 reasoning questions to determine retry strategy.
   337→
   338→    Called by ground_node when an entity fails initial grounding (zero
   339→    candidates or confidence < 0.5). Asks MedGemma in a single prompt:
   340→    - Q1: Is this a valid medical criterion (or should it be skipped)?
   341→    - Q2: Is this a derived entity that maps to a standard concept?
   342→    - Q3: Can this entity be rephrased for better terminology search?
   343→
   344→    Uses the two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical reasoning, Gemini
   345→    for structured output parsing (and optional reformulation suggestion).
   346→
   347→    Args:
   348→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   349→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   350→        router: TerminologyRouter instance (for get_apis_for_entity context).
   351→        attempt: Current attempt number (1-3) for prompt context.
   352→
   353→    Returns:
   354→        AgenticReasoningResult with should_skip, is_derived, derived_term,
   355→        rephrased_query, gemini_suggestion, and reasoning fields.
   356→        On error, returns default result (no skip, no rephrase) to allow
   357→        the retry loop to continue with the original query.
   358→    """
   359→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   360→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   361→    previous_query = entity.get("_previous_query", entity_text)
   362→
   363→    logger.info(
   364→        "Agentic reasoning (attempt %d) for entity '%s' (type=%s)",
   365→        attempt,
   366→        entity_text[:50],
   367→        entity_type,
   368→    )
   369→
   370→    try:
   371→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   372→
   373→        system_prompt = render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   374→        reasoning_prompt = render_template(
   375→            "grounding_reasoning.jinja2",
   376→            entity_text=entity_text,
   377→            entity_type=entity_type,
   378→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   379→            previous_query=previous_query,
   380→            attempt=attempt,
   381→        )
   382→
   383→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   384→
   385→        model_name = getattr(model, "model_name", "") or getattr(model, "model", "")
   386→        messages = [
   387→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   388→            HumanMessage(content=reasoning_prompt),
   389→        ]
   390→
   391→        with llm_span("medgemma_reasoning", str(model_name)) as llm:
   392→            llm.set_request(f"[system] {system_prompt}\n\n[user] {reasoning_prompt}")
   393→            raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   394→            raw_text = raw_response.content
   395→            usage_r: dict[str, int] = {}
   396→            um_r = getattr(raw_response, "usage_metadata", None)
   397→            if isinstance(um_r, dict):
   398→                if um_r.get("input_tokens"):
   399→                    usage_r["input_tokens"] = um_r["input_tokens"]
   400→                if um_r.get("output_tokens"):
   401→                    usage_r["output_tokens"] = um_r["output_tokens"]
   402→                if um_r.get("total_tokens"):
   403→                    usage_r["total_tokens"] = um_r["total_tokens"]
   404→            llm.set_response(str(raw_text), usage_r or None)
   405→
   406→        logger.debug(
   407→            "MedGemma reasoning response for '%s' (first 300 chars): %s",
   408→            entity_text[:30],
   409→            raw_text[:300],
   410→        )
   411→
   412→        result = await _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text)
   413→
   414→        logger.info(
   415→            "Agentic reasoning result for '%s': skip=%s, derived=%s, "
   416→            "derived_term=%s, rephrased=%s",
   417→            entity_text[:50],
   418→            result.should_skip,
   419→            result.is_derived,
   420→            result.derived_term,
   421→            result.rephrased_query,
   422→        )
   423→
   424→        return result
   425→
   426→    except Exception as e:
   427→        logger.error(
   428→            "Agentic reasoning loop failed for entity '%s': %s",
   429→            entity_text[:50],
   430→            e,
   431→            exc_info=True,
   432→        )
   433→        # Return default result (no skip, no rephrase) — retry with original query
   434→        return AgenticReasoningResult(
   435→            should_skip=False,
   436→            is_derived=False,
   437→            derived_term=None,
   438→            rephrased_query=None,
   439→            reasoning=f"Reasoning loop failed: {e}",
   440→        )
   441→

     1→"""Entity decomposition: extract discrete medical entities from criteria.
     2→
     3→Criterion sentences like "eGFR >= 30 mL/min/1.73m2 or sCr <= 2.0 mg/dL" contain
     4→multiple groundable medical concepts. This tool decomposes them into discrete
     5→entities with correct types for TerminologyRouter dispatch.
     6→
     7→Uses the two-model architecture pattern: Gemini for structured output via
     8→LangChain's with_structured_output, matching the medgemma_decider.py pattern.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import logging
    14→import os
    15→from typing import Any, Literal, cast
    16→
    17→from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    18→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.prompts import render_template
    21→
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→# Unicode math symbols that Gemini/PDF extraction may produce.
    25→# Normalize to ASCII equivalents so the LLM template handles them cleanly.
    26→_UNICODE_OPERATORS: dict[str, str] = {
    27→    "\u2265": ">=",  # ≥
    28→    "\u2264": "<=",  # ≤
    29→    "\u2260": "!=",  # ≠
    30→    "\u00b1": "+-",  # ±
    31→    "\u2013": "-",  # en-dash
    32→    "\u2014": "-",  # em-dash
    33→}
    34→
    35→
    36→def _normalize_criterion_text(text: str) -> str:
    37→    """Replace unicode math/comparison symbols with ASCII equivalents."""
    38→    for char, repl in _UNICODE_OPERATORS.items():
    39→        text = text.replace(char, repl)
    40→    return text
    41→
    42→
    43→class DecomposedEntity(BaseModel):
    44→    """A single medical entity extracted from a criterion sentence.
    45→
    46→    Attributes:
    47→        text: The specific medical term to ground (e.g. "eGFR", not the full sentence).
    48→        entity_type: Entity type matching routing.yaml keys exactly.
    49→    """
    50→
    51→    text: str = Field(description="The specific medical term to ground")
    52→    entity_type: Literal[
    53→        "Condition", "Medication", "Lab_Value", "Procedure", "Demographic", "Other"
    54→    ] = Field(description="Entity type for terminology routing")
    55→
    56→
    57→class DecomposedEntityList(BaseModel):
    58→    """List of decomposed entities from a single criterion sentence."""
    59→
    60→    entities: list[DecomposedEntity] = Field(default_factory=list)
    61→
    62→
    63→def _rephrase_for_retry(text: str, attempt: int) -> str:
    64→    """Apply small wording changes for retry attempts.
    65→
    66→    Each attempt surfaces the same medical content with slightly
    67→    different phrasing so the LLM gets a fresh chance to extract entities.
    68→    """
    69→    if attempt == 1:
    70→        return f"Eligibility criterion: {text}. Identify every medical concept."
    71→    # attempt == 2
    72→    return f"Clinical trial requirement — {text}. What medical entities are mentioned?"
    73→
    74→
    75→async def _invoke_decompose(
    76→    structured: Any,
    77→    prompt: str,
    78→    label: str,
    79→    model_name: str,
    80→) -> DecomposedEntityList:
    81→    """Single invocation of the structured LLM with tracing."""
    82→    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
    83→
    84→    with llm_span(label, model_name) as llm:
    85→        llm.set_request(prompt)
    86→        result = await structured.ainvoke(prompt)
    87→        llm.set_response(str(result))
    88→
    89→    if isinstance(result, dict):
    90→        result = DecomposedEntityList.model_validate(result)
    91→    return cast(DecomposedEntityList, result)
    92→
    93→
    94→async def decompose_entities_from_criterion(
    95→    criterion_text: str,
    96→    category: str | None,
    97→    *,
    98→    max_retries: int = 2,
    99→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   100→    """Extract discrete medical entities from a criterion sentence.
   101→
   102→    Uses Gemini with structured output to decompose a criterion sentence
   103→    into individual medical terms with correct entity types. If the first
   104→    attempt returns zero entities, retries up to *max_retries* times with
   105→    small wording changes before returning an empty list.
   106→
   107→    Args:
   108→        criterion_text: The full criterion sentence to decompose.
   109→        category: Optional category hint from extraction.
   110→        max_retries: Number of retry attempts with rephrased wording (default 2).
   111→
   112→    Returns:
   113→        List of dicts with "text" and "entity_type" keys, or empty list on failure.
   114→    """
   115→    # Normalize unicode operators (≥, ≤, etc.) to ASCII before LLM call
   116→    normalized_text = _normalize_criterion_text(criterion_text)
   117→
   118→    try:
   119→        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   120→            model=os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash"),
   121→            google_api_key=os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY"),
   122→            max_output_tokens=2048,
   123→        )
   124→        model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   125→        structured = gemini.with_structured_output(DecomposedEntityList)
   126→
   127→        # Attempt 0: original prompt
   128→        prompt = render_template(
   129→            "entity_decompose.jinja2",
   130→            criterion_text=normalized_text,
   131→            category=category or "",
   132→        )
   133→        decomposed = await _invoke_decompose(
   134→            structured,
   135→            prompt,
   136→            "gemini_entity_decompose",
   137→            model_name,
   138→        )
   139→
   140→        # Retry with rephrased wording if we got 0 entities
   141→        for attempt in range(1, max_retries + 1):
   142→            if decomposed.entities:
   143→                break
   144→            rephrased = _rephrase_for_retry(normalized_text, attempt)
   145→            retry_prompt = render_template(
   146→                "entity_decompose.jinja2",
   147→                criterion_text=rephrased,
   148→                category=category or "",
   149→            )
   150→            logger.info(
   151→                "Entity decomposition retry %d/%d for '%s'",
   152→                attempt,
   153→                max_retries,
   154→                normalized_text[:60],
   155→            )
   156→            decomposed = await _invoke_decompose(
   157→                structured,
   158→                retry_prompt,
   159→                f"gemini_entity_decompose_retry{attempt}",
   160→                model_name,
   161→            )
   162→
   163→        if not decomposed.entities:
   164→            logger.warning(
   165→                "Entity decomposition returned 0 entities after %d retries for '%s'",
   166→                max_retries,
   167→                normalized_text[:80],
   168→            )
   169→
   170→        return [e.model_dump() for e in decomposed.entities]
   171→    except Exception as e:
   172→        logger.error(
   173→            "Entity decomposition failed for criterion '%s': %s",
   174→            normalized_text[:80],
   175→            e,
   176→            exc_info=True,
   177→        )
   178→        return []
   179→
   180→
   181→async def medgemma_decompose_entities(
   182→    criterion_text: str,
   183→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   184→    """Fallback: ask MedGemma to identify medical entities when Gemini fails.
   185→
   186→    Uses MedGemma (medical expert) to extract entity names, then Gemini to
   187→    structure the output into typed entities. Called by parse_node when
   188→    Gemini decomposition returns empty even after retries.
   189→
   190→    Args:
   191→        criterion_text: The criterion sentence to decompose.
   192→
   193→    Returns:
   194→        List of dicts with "text" and "entity_type" keys, or empty list on failure.
   195→    """
   196→    normalized_text = _normalize_criterion_text(criterion_text)
   197→
   198→    try:
   199→        from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, SystemMessage
   200→
   201→        from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import _get_medgemma_model
   202→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   203→
   204→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   205→        model_name = getattr(model, "model_name", "") or getattr(model, "model", "")
   206→
   207→        messages = [
   208→            SystemMessage(content=render_template("medgemma_decompose_system.jinja2")),
   209→            HumanMessage(
   210→                content=render_template(
   211→                    "medgemma_decompose_user.jinja2",
   212→                    criterion_text=normalized_text,
   213→                )
   214→            ),
   215→        ]
   216→
   217→        with llm_span("medgemma_entity_decompose", str(model_name)) as llm:
   218→            llm.set_request(str(messages))
   219→            raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   220→            raw_text = raw_response.content
   221→            llm.set_response(str(raw_text))
   222→
   223→        # Use Gemini to structure MedGemma's free-text into typed entities
   224→        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   225→            model=os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash"),
   226→            google_api_key=os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY"),
   227→            max_output_tokens=2048,
   228→        )
   229→        structured = gemini.with_structured_output(DecomposedEntityList)
   230→
   231→        structure_prompt = render_template(
   232→            "structure_entities.jinja2", raw_text=raw_text
   233→        )
   234→        gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   235→
   236→        with llm_span("gemini_structure_medgemma_decompose", gemini_model_name) as llm:
   237→            llm.set_request(structure_prompt)
   238→            result = await structured.ainvoke(structure_prompt)
   239→            llm.set_response(str(result))
   240→
   241→        if isinstance(result, dict):
   242→            result = DecomposedEntityList.model_validate(result)
   243→        decomposed = cast(DecomposedEntityList, result)
   244→
   245→        if decomposed.entities:
   246→            logger.info(
   247→                "MedGemma decomposition found %d entities for '%s'",
   248→                len(decomposed.entities),
   249→                normalized_text[:60],
   250→            )
   251→        else:
   252→            logger.warning(
   253→                "MedGemma decomposition also returned 0 entities for '%s'",
   254→                normalized_text[:80],
   255→            )
   256→
   257→        return [e.model_dump() for e in decomposed.entities]
   258→
   259→    except Exception as e:
   260→        logger.error(
   261→            "MedGemma entity decomposition failed for '%s': %s",
   262→            criterion_text[:80],
   263→            e,
   264→            exc_info=True,
   265→        )
   266→        return []
   267→
```

  "old_string": "async def _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> GroundingDecision:\n    \"\"\"Structure raw MedGemma output using Gemini with_structured_output.\n\n    Args:\n        raw_text: Raw MedGemma output (free-form medical reasoning).\n\n    Returns:\n        GroundingDecision with selected code and confidence.\n    \"\"\"\n    gemini_model_name = os.getenv(\"GEMINI_MODEL_NAME\", \"gemini-2.5-flash\")\n    google_api_key = os.getenv(\"GOOGLE_API_KEY\")\n\n    if not google_api_key:\n        raise ValueError(\"GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required\")\n\n    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span\n\n    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(\n        model=gemini_model_name,\n        google_api_key=google_api_key,\n        max_output_tokens=2048,\n    )\n    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(GroundingDecision)\n\n    prompt = render_template(\"structure_decision.jinja2\", raw_text=raw_text)\n\n    with llm_span(\"gemini_structure_decision\", gemini_model_name) as llm:\n        llm.set_request(prompt)\n        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)\n        resp_text = str(result) if not isinstance(result, dict) else str(result)\n        llm.set_response(resp_text)\n\n    if isinstance(result, dict):\n        return GroundingDecision.model_validate(result)\n    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]",
  "new_string": "async def _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> GroundingDecision:\n    \"\"\"Structure raw MedGemma output using Gemini with_structured_output.\n\n    Args:\n        raw_text: Raw MedGemma output (free-form medical reasoning).\n\n    Returns:\n        GroundingDecision with selected code and confidence.\n    \"\"\"\n    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(GroundingDecision)\n    if structured_llm is None:\n        raise ValueError(\"No LLM backend available (GOOGLE_API_KEY or OLLAMA_BASE_URL required)\")\n\n    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span\n\n    prompt = render_template(\"structure_decision.jinja2\", raw_text=raw_text)\n\n    gemini_model_name = os.getenv(\"GEMINI_MODEL_NAME\", \"gemini-2.5-flash\")\n    with llm_span(\"gemini_structure_decision\", gemini_model_name) as llm:\n        llm.set_request(prompt)\n        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)\n        llm.set_response(str(result))\n\n    return parse_structured_output(result, GroundingDecision)"

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/medgemma_decider.py",
  "old_string": "async def _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> AgenticReasoningResult:\n    \"\"\"Structure raw MedGemma reasoning output using Gemini with_structured_output.\n\n    Gemini also acts as a collaborating agent here — it can add its own\n    reformulation suggestions via the gemini_suggestion field when structuring\n    the output.\n\n    Args:\n        raw_text: Raw MedGemma reasoning output (free-form text).\n\n    Returns:\n        AgenticReasoningResult with structured 3-question answers.\n    \"\"\"\n    gemini_model_name = os.getenv(\"GEMINI_MODEL_NAME\", \"gemini-2.5-flash\")\n    google_api_key = os.getenv(\"GOOGLE_API_KEY\")\n\n    if not google_api_key:\n        raise ValueError(\"GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required\")\n\n    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span\n\n    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(\n        model=gemini_model_name,\n        google_api_key=google_api_key,\n        max_output_tokens=2048,\n    )\n    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(AgenticReasoningResult)\n\n    prompt = render_template(\"structure_reasoning.jinja2\", raw_text=raw_text)\n\n    with llm_span(\"gemini_structure_reasoning\", gemini_model_name) as llm:\n        llm.set_request(prompt)\n        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)\n        llm.set_response(str(result))\n\n    if isinstance(result, dict):\n        return AgenticReasoningResult.model_validate(result)\n    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]",
  "new_string": "async def _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> AgenticReasoningResult:\n    \"\"\"Structure raw MedGemma reasoning output using Gemini with_structured_output.\n\n    Gemini also acts as a collaborating agent here — it can add its own\n    reformulation suggestions via the gemini_suggestion field when structuring\n    the output.\n\n    Args:\n        raw_text: Raw MedGemma reasoning output (free-form text).\n\n    Returns:\n        AgenticReasoningResult with structured 3-question answers.\n    \"\"\"\n    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(AgenticReasoningResult)\n    if structured_llm is None:\n        raise ValueError(\"No LLM backend available (GOOGLE_API_KEY or OLLAMA_BASE_URL required)\")\n\n    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span\n\n    prompt = render_template(\"structure_reasoning.jinja2\", raw_text=raw_text)\n\n    gemini_model_name = os.getenv(\"GEMINI_MODEL_NAME\", \"gemini-2.5-flash\")\n    with llm_span(\"gemini_structure_reasoning\", gemini_model_name) as llm:\n        llm.set_request(prompt)\n        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)\n        llm.set_response(str(result))\n\n    return parse_structured_output(result, AgenticReasoningResult)"

     1→"""Quick validation of repetition loop detection and sanitization."""
     2→
     3→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
     4→    is_repetition_loop,

     6→)
     7→from protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper import FieldMappingValue, FieldMappingResponse, FieldMappingItem

    10→def test_detection():
    11→    """Test is_repetition_loop() against known patterns."""
    12→    test_cases = [
    13→        ("2202202020202020202020202020202020", True, "repeating digits"),
    14→        (
    15→            "8598739459392231e-3158739459392231e-3158739459392231e-315",
    16→            True,
    17→            "repeating sci notation",
    18→        ),
    19→        ("2Unit of measurement (e.g. years)", True, "schema leak"),
    20→        ("Value for standard type", True, "schema leak 2"),
    21→        ("Duration value for temporal type", True, "schema leak 3"),
    22→        ("True", False, "normal boolean"),
    23→        ("7", False, "normal number"),
    24→        ("%", False, "normal unit"),
    25→        ("mg/dL", False, "normal unit 2"),
    26→        ("HbA1c", False, "normal entity"),
    27→        ("44", False, "normal value"),
    28→    ]
    29→
    30→    print("=== is_repetition_loop() unit tests ===")
    31→    all_pass = True
    32→    for text, expected, label in test_cases:
    33→        result = is_repetition_loop(text)
    34→        status = "OK" if result == expected else "FAIL"
    35→        if status == "FAIL":
    36→            all_pass = False
    37→        print(f"  {status}: {label:30s} | expected={expected}, got={result}")
    38→
    39→    print()
    40→    return all_pass
    41→
    42→
    43→def test_sanitization():
    44→    """Test FieldMappingValue validator nulls out repetitive values."""
    45→    print("=== FieldMappingValue sanitization ===")
    46→
    47→    # Repetitive value + schema-leak unit should both be nulled
    48→    val = FieldMappingValue(
    49→        type="standard",
    50→        value="2202202020202020202020202020202020",
    51→        unit="Unit of measurement (e.g. years)",
    52→    )
    53→    print(f"  value after sanitization: {val.value!r} (expected None)")
    54→    print(f"  unit after sanitization:  {val.unit!r} (expected None)")
    55→    assert val.value is None, f"Expected None, got {val.value!r}"
    56→    assert val.unit is None, f"Expected None, got {val.unit!r}"
    57→
    58→    # Normal values should be preserved
    59→    val2 = FieldMappingValue(type="standard", value="7", unit="%")
    60→    print(f"  normal value preserved:   {val2.value!r} (expected '7')")
    61→    print(f"  normal unit preserved:    {val2.unit!r} (expected '%')")
    62→    assert val2.value == "7"
    63→    assert val2.unit == "%"
    64→
    65→    print()
    66→    return True
    67→
    68→
    69→def test_model_check():
    70→    """Test check_model_for_repetition() on a full response."""
    71→    print("=== check_model_for_repetition() ===")
    72→
    73→    # Response with one clean mapping and one repetitive mapping
    74→    response = FieldMappingResponse(
    75→        mappings=[
    76→            FieldMappingItem(
    77→                entity="HbA1c",
    78→                relation="<",
    79→                value=FieldMappingValue(type="standard", value="7", unit="%"),
    80→            ),
    81→            FieldMappingItem(
    82→                entity="Postmenopause",
    83→                relation="=",
    84→                value=FieldMappingValue(
    85→                    type="standard",
    86→                    value="2202202020202020202020202020202020",
    87→                    unit=None,
    88→                ),
    89→            ),
    90→        ]
    91→    )
    92→
    93→    # Note: the validator will have already nulled out the repetitive value
    94→    # during Pydantic construction. So check_model_for_repetition should
    95→    # find nothing (since the validator cleaned it). Let's verify.
    96→    bad = check_model_for_repetition(response)
    97→    print(f"  Bad fields after Pydantic sanitization: {bad}")
    98→    print(f"  (expected empty — validator already cleaned)")
    99→    print(f"  Mapping[1] value.value = {response.mappings[1].value.value!r}")
   100→
   101→    # Now test with a raw dict that bypasses the validator
   102→    # (simulating what check_model_for_repetition would see BEFORE validation)
   103→    from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
   104→
   105→    class RawValue(BaseModel):
   106→        type: str = "standard"
   107→        value: str | None = None
   108→
   109→    class RawMapping(BaseModel):
   110→        entity: str = ""
   111→        mappings: list[RawValue] = Field(default_factory=list)
   112→
   113→    raw = RawMapping(
   114→        mappings=[
   115→            RawValue(value="ok"),
   116→            RawValue(value="220220220220220220220220220220220220"),
   117→        ]
   118→    )
   119→    bad2 = check_model_for_repetition(raw)
   120→    print(f"  Raw model bad fields: {bad2}")
   121→    assert len(bad2) == 1, f"Expected 1 bad field, got {bad2}"
   122→
   123→    print()
   124→    return True
   125→
   126→
   127→if __name__ == "__main__":
   128→    p1 = test_detection()
   129→    p2 = test_sanitization()
   130→    p3 = test_model_check()
   131→
   132→    if p1 and p2 and p3:
   133→        print("ALL TESTS PASSED")
   134→    else:
   135→        print("SOME TESTS FAILED")
   136→        exit(1)
   137→

Here's a summary of what was changed across the 4 layers:

**Layer 1 — Consolidated all Gemini structured output through `create_structured_llm()`:**
- `medgemma_decider.py`: Replaced 2 inline `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` blocks in `_structure_decision_with_gemini()` and `_structure_reasoning_with_gemini()` with `create_structured_llm()`. Removed `langchain_google_genai` import.
- `entity_decomposer.py`: Replaced 2 inline `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` blocks in `decompose_entities_from_criterion()` and `medgemma_decompose_entities()` with `create_structured_llm()`. Removed `langchain_google_genai` import.

**Layer 2 — Fixed generation parameters in `create_structured_llm()`:**
- `gemini_utils.py`: Added `temperature=0.1`, `frequency_penalty=0.8`, `presence_penalty=0.5` — now applies to all 5 callsites.

**Layer 3 — Made all Pydantic schema fields required:**
- `field_mapper.py`: Changed all `Field(default=None)` to `Field(...)` and `Field(default_factory=list)` to `Field(...)`. All 3 models now have every field in their JSON schema `required` array.

**Layer 4 — Safety net already in place** (detection + retry kept as-is).

**Test fix:** Updated `test_repetition_detection.py` to pass required `None` values explicitly.

     1→"""E2E: Validate grounding against test_snippets.json golden examples.
     2→
     3→Requires the full Docker Compose stack to be running. Tests are auto-skipped
     4→when the stack is unavailable.
     5→
     6→Extraction snippets: verify classification (inclusion/exclusion/neither).
     7→Grounding snippets: verify entity names, terminology system codes, and
     8→relation operators match expected values.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import json
    14→from pathlib import Path
    15→
    16→import pytest
    17→
    18→_SNIPPETS_PATH = Path(__file__).parent / "test_snippets.json"
    19→
    20→
    21→def _snippet_counts() -> tuple[int, int]:
    22→    """Load snippet counts for parametrization (avoids hardcoded range)."""
    23→    data = json.loads(_SNIPPETS_PATH.read_text())
    24→    return (
    25→        len(data["extraction_test_snippets"]),
    26→        len(data["grounding_test_snippets"]),
    27→    )
    28→
    29→
    30→_NUM_EXTRACTION, _NUM_GROUNDING = _snippet_counts()
    31→
    32→
    33→@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
    34→def snippets_data() -> dict:
    35→    """Load the test_snippets.json fixture."""
    36→    return json.loads(_SNIPPETS_PATH.read_text())
    37→
    38→
    39→@pytest.fixture()
    40→def extraction_snippets(snippets_data: dict) -> list[dict]:
    41→    return snippets_data["extraction_test_snippets"]
    42→
    43→
    44→@pytest.fixture()
    45→def grounding_snippets(snippets_data: dict) -> list[dict]:
    46→    return snippets_data["grounding_test_snippets"]
    47→
    48→
    49→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    50→# Extraction tests — classification validation
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→
    53→
    54→@pytest.mark.e2e
    55→class TestExtractionSnippets:
    56→    """Validate extraction snippet golden classification."""
    57→
    58→    @pytest.mark.parametrize("idx", range(_NUM_EXTRACTION))
    59→    def test_extraction_snippet_structure(
    60→        self, idx: int, extraction_snippets: list[dict]
    61→    ) -> None:
    62→        """Each extraction snippet has required fields."""
    63→        snippet = extraction_snippets[idx]
    64→        assert "snippet_text" in snippet
    65→        assert "classification" in snippet
    66→        assert snippet["classification"] in ("inclusion", "exclusion", "neither")
    67→
    68→    @pytest.mark.parametrize("idx", range(_NUM_EXTRACTION))
    69→    def test_extraction_snippet_criteria_consistency(
    70→        self, idx: int, extraction_snippets: list[dict]
    71→    ) -> None:
    72→        """'neither' snippets have null extracted_criteria; others have text."""
    73→        snippet = extraction_snippets[idx]
    74→        if snippet["classification"] == "neither":
    75→            assert snippet["extracted_criteria"] is None
    76→        else:
    77→            assert snippet["extracted_criteria"] is not None
    78→            assert len(snippet["extracted_criteria"]) > 0
    79→
    80→
    81→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    82→# Grounding tests — entity/code/relation validation
    83→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    84→
    85→# Normalize legacy relation operators the same way the pipeline does
    86→_RELATION_NORMALIZATIONS = {
    87→    "==": "=",
    88→    "is": "=",
    89→    "has": "contains",
    90→    "not": "not_contains",
    91→}
    92→
    93→
    94→@pytest.mark.e2e
    95→class TestGroundingSnippets:
    96→    """Validate grounding snippet golden entity examples."""
    97→
    98→    @pytest.mark.parametrize("idx", range(_NUM_GROUNDING))
    99→    def test_grounding_snippet_has_entities(
   100→        self, idx: int, grounding_snippets: list[dict]
   101→    ) -> None:
   102→        """Each grounding snippet has at least one entity."""
   103→        snippet = grounding_snippets[idx]
   104→        assert "entities" in snippet
   105→        assert len(snippet["entities"]) > 0
   106→
   107→    @pytest.mark.parametrize("idx", range(_NUM_GROUNDING))
   108→    def test_grounding_snippet_entity_fields(
   109→        self, idx: int, grounding_snippets: list[dict]
   110→    ) -> None:
   111→        """Each entity has required fields including unit terminology fields."""
   112→        snippet = grounding_snippets[idx]
   113→        for entity in snippet["entities"]:
   114→            assert "entity_name" in entity
   115→            assert "system" in entity
   116→            assert "code" in entity
   117→            assert "relation" in entity
   118→            assert "value" in entity
   119→            assert "unit" in entity
   120→            assert "unit_ucum" in entity
   121→            assert "unit_omop_concept_id" in entity
   122→
   123→    @pytest.mark.parametrize("idx", range(_NUM_GROUNDING))
   124→    def test_grounding_snippet_system_is_valid(
   125→        self, idx: int, grounding_snippets: list[dict]
   126→    ) -> None:
   127→        """All golden entities use a recognized terminology system."""
   128→        valid_systems = {"UMLS", "SNOMED", "ICD10", "LOINC", "RxNorm", "HPO"}
   129→        snippet = grounding_snippets[idx]
   130→        for entity in snippet["entities"]:
   131→            assert entity["system"] in valid_systems, (
   132→                f"Unknown system '{entity['system']}'"
   133→            )
   134→
   135→    @pytest.mark.parametrize("idx", range(_NUM_GROUNDING))
   136→    def test_grounding_snippet_codes_are_cui_format(
   137→        self, idx: int, grounding_snippets: list[dict]
   138→    ) -> None:
   139→        """All codes start with 'C' followed by digits (CUI format from UMLS/SNOMED)."""
   140→        snippet = grounding_snippets[idx]
   141→        for entity in snippet["entities"]:
   142→            code = entity["code"]
   143→            assert code.startswith("C"), f"Expected CUI format, got: {code}"
   144→            assert code[1:].isdigit(), f"Expected CUI digits after 'C', got: {code}"
   145→
   146→    @pytest.mark.parametrize("idx", range(_NUM_GROUNDING))
   147→    def test_grounding_snippet_relations_are_valid(
   148→        self, idx: int, grounding_snippets: list[dict]
   149→    ) -> None:
   150→        """All relations are valid operator strings (after normalization)."""
   151→        valid_relations = {
   152→            "<",
   153→            "<=",
   154→            ">",
   155→            ">=",
   156→            "=",
   157→            "!=",
   158→            "contains",
   159→            "not_contains",
   160→            "within",
   161→            "==",
   162→        }
   163→        snippet = grounding_snippets[idx]
   164→        for entity in snippet["entities"]:
   165→            rel = entity["relation"]
   166→            normalized = _RELATION_NORMALIZATIONS.get(rel, rel)
   167→            assert normalized in valid_relations, (
   168→                f"Invalid relation '{rel}' (normalized: '{normalized}')"
   169→            )
   170→
   171→    @pytest.mark.parametrize("idx", range(_NUM_GROUNDING))
   172→    def test_grounding_snippet_unit_consistency(
   173→        self, idx: int, grounding_snippets: list[dict]
   174→    ) -> None:
   175→        """Unit fields are internally consistent.
   176→
   177→        - When unit is null, unit_ucum and unit_omop_concept_id must both be null.
   178→        - When unit_ucum is non-null, unit_omop_concept_id must also be non-null.
   179→        """
   180→        snippet = grounding_snippets[idx]
   181→        for entity in snippet["entities"]:
   182→            if entity["unit"] is None:
   183→                assert entity["unit_ucum"] is None, (
   184→                    f"unit is null but unit_ucum is {entity['unit_ucum']}"
   185→                )
   186→                assert entity["unit_omop_concept_id"] is None, (
   187→                    f"unit is null but unit_omop_concept_id is "
   188→                    f"{entity['unit_omop_concept_id']}"
   189→                )
   190→            if entity["unit_ucum"] is not None:
   191→                assert entity["unit_omop_concept_id"] is not None, (
   192→                    f"unit_ucum is {entity['unit_ucum']} but "
   193→                    f"unit_omop_concept_id is null"
   194→                )
   195→
